# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some Oxlint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the Oxlint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend enabling type-aware lint rules by installing `oxlint-tsgolint` and editing `.oxlintrc.json`:

```json
{
  "$schema": "./node_modules/oxlint/configuration_schema.json",
  "plugins": ["react", "typescript", "oxc"],
  "options": {
    "typeAware": true
  },
  "rules": {
    "react/rules-of-hooks": "error",
    "react/only-export-components": ["warn", { "allowConstantExport": true }]
  }
}
```

See the [Oxlint rules documentation](https://oxc.rs/docs/guide/usage/linter/rules) for the full list of rules and categories.

==================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import Header from './components/layout/Header';
import Sidebar from './components/layout/Sidebar';
import CommandPalette from './components/layout/CommandPalette';
import DashboardPage from './pages/DashboardPage';
import HoneypotLogsPage from './pages/HoneypotLogsPage';
import IocPage from './pages/IocPage';
import InvestigationPage from './pages/InvestigationPage';
import CvePage from './pages/CvePage';
import ActorsPage from './pages/ActorsPage';
import NewsPage from './pages/NewsPage';
import RulesPage from './pages/RulesPage';
import MitreNavigatorPage from './pages/MitreNavigatorPage';
import VulnerabilityManagerPage from './pages/VulnerabilityManagerPage';
import PlaybookPage from './pages/PlaybookPage';
import PlaceholderPage from './pages/PlaceholderPage';
import {
  Crosshair, Network, FileText, Briefcase, Share2, HardDrive,
  PencilLine, Zap, Box, Monitor, Shield, Bug, Users, Newspaper,
  Skull, Fingerprint, Globe, Mail, Search, Target, Book, Layers,
  Server, Archive, MessageSquare, HelpCircle, Settings,
  MonitorPlay, CloudCog, ShieldAlert,
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function App() {
  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem('at_view') || 'dashboard';
  });
  const [sidebarCollapsed, setSidebarCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false);

  const navigate = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setActiveView(id);
    localStorage.setItem('at_view', id);
  }, []);

  // Ctrl+K handler
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSearchOpen(prev => !prev);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, []);

  const renderPage = () => {
    switch (activeView) {
      case 'dashboard':
        return <DashboardPage onNavigate={navigate} />;
      case 'honeypot_logs':
        return <HoneypotLogsPage onNavigate={navigate} />;
      case 'ioc':
        return <IocPage />;

      // Placeholders for all other views
      case 'soc_wall':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="SOC Wall" description="Full-screen cinematic visualization for wall displays. Auto-refreshing attack map, critical activity, and threat counters." icon={MonitorPlay} />;
      case 'attack_map':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Attack Map" description="Geospatial visualization of active attacks against honeypot infrastructure." icon={Crosshair} />;
      case 'infrastructure':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Infrastructure" description="Manage servers, agents, log shippers, containers, and honeypot deployments." icon={CloudCog} />;
      case 'analysis':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Analysis" description="Parse, normalize, and score raw server logs with signature matching and enrichment." icon={FileText} />;
      case 'investigation':
        return <InvestigationPage />;
      case 'investigation_bench':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Investigation Bench" description="Case management workspace with evidence, timeline, and audit history." icon={Briefcase} />;
      case 'network':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Network Forensics" description="Analyze PCAP files, TCP streams, and packet headers." icon={Network} />;
      case 'topology':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Network Topology" description="Security-focused network builder with attack path simulation." icon={Share2} />;
      case 'nettools':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Network Tools" description="DNS lookup, Whois, Traceroute, port scanning, and certificate analysis." icon={HardDrive} />;
      case 'threat_canvas':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Threat Canvas" description="Interactive STRIDE/PASTA/LINDDUN threat modeling with AI analysis." icon={PencilLine} />;
      case 'cyberchef':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="CyberChef" description="Tactical data transformation, encoding/decoding, and hash generation." icon={Zap} />;
      case 'dynamic_sandbox':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Dynamic Sandbox" description="Detonate files and URLs in a secure isolated environment." icon={Box} />;
      case 'sandbox_browser':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Sandbox Browser" description="Secure web browser for inspecting potentially malicious websites." icon={Monitor} />;
      case 'cve':
        return <CvePage />;
      case 'exploit':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Exploit Database" description="Database of public exploits, proof-of-concepts, and weaponized vulnerabilities." icon={Bug} />;
      case 'actors':
        return <ActorsPage />;
      case 'news':
        return <NewsPage />;
      case 'ransomware':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Ransomware Monitor" description="Track active ransomware groups, leak sites, and recent victims." icon={Skull} />;
      case 'brand_monitor':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Brand Monitor" description="Monitor brand impersonation, typosquatting, and domain abuse." icon={Fingerprint} />;
      case 'webcheck':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Web Check" description="Deep website analysis including headers, certificates, and technologies." icon={Globe} />;
      case 'email_forensic':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Email Forensic" description="Analyze email headers, authentication, and routing hops." icon={Mail} />;
      case 'intel_search':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Intel Search" description="Deep research tool using AI-powered search grounding." icon={Search} />;
      case 'recon':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Recon" description="Attack surface reconnaissance â€” targets, assets, vulnerabilities, and exposure." icon={Target} />;
      case 'rules':
        return <RulesPage />;
      case 'mitre':
        return <MitreNavigatorPage />;
      case 'vuln_manager':
        return <VulnerabilityManagerPage />;
      case 'playbook':
        return <PlaybookPage />;
      case 'chat':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="AI Assistant" description="Interact with the integrated AI security analyst for threat analysis." icon={MessageSquare} />;
      case 'help':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Help & Documentation" description="Keyboard shortcuts, user guide, API documentation, and getting started." icon={HelpCircle} />;
      case 'settings':
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Settings" description="Application settings, API keys, notifications, and preferences." icon={Settings} />;
      default:
        return <PlaceholderPage title="Page Not Found" description="This page does not exist." />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-screen flex flex-col bg-at-bg overflow-hidden">
      <div className="at-grid-bg" />

      <Header onOpenSearch={() => setSearchOpen(true)} />

      <div className="flex flex-1 min-h-0 relative z-10">
        <Sidebar
          activeView={activeView}
          onNavigate={navigate}
          collapsed={sidebarCollapsed}
          onToggleCollapse={() => setSidebarCollapsed(!sidebarCollapsed)}
        />

        <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 min-w-0 bg-at-bg">
          {renderPage()}
        </main>
      </div>

      <CommandPalette
        isOpen={searchOpen}
        onClose={() => setSearchOpen(false)}
        onNavigate={navigate}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

File: C:\Users\rishi\ANTI AI\ANTI-TODE\Xyberah-redesign\src\components\layout\CommandPalette.tsx
==================================================
import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Search, X, Clock, ArrowRight, Hash, Globe, Server, Shield, Users, Command, MonitorPlay, Terminal } from 'lucide-react';
import { TOP_LEVEL_ITEMS, NAV_GROUPS, UTILITY_ITEMS, BOTTOM_ITEMS } from '../../data/navigation';

interface CommandPaletteProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onNavigate: (id: string) => void;
}

const ENTITY_TYPES = [
  { icon: Server, label: 'IP Address', example: 'ip:192.168.1.1' },
  { icon: Globe, label: 'Domain', example: 'domain:evil.com' },
  { icon: Hash, label: 'Hash', example: 'hash:275a021b...' },
  { icon: Shield, label: 'CVE', example: 'cve:CVE-2024-1234' },
  { icon: Users, label: 'Actor', example: 'actor:APT28' },
];

const RECENT_SEARCHES = [
  '185.220.101.42', 'CVE-2024-21762', 'evil.ru', 'APT29', '45.33.32.156',
];

const SUGGESTED_COMMANDS = [
  { icon: MonitorPlay, label: 'Enter SOC Wall Mode', cmd: '> soc_wall' },
  { icon: Terminal, label: 'Open CyberChef', cmd: '> cyberchef' },
  { icon: Shield, label: 'Run MITRE Analysis', cmd: '> mitre' }
];

export default function CommandPalette({ isOpen, onClose, onNavigate }: CommandPaletteProps) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState(0);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  
  const allNavItems = useMemo(() => [
    ...TOP_LEVEL_ITEMS,
    ...NAV_GROUPS.flatMap(g => g.items),
    ...UTILITY_ITEMS,
    ...BOTTOM_ITEMS
  ], []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setQuery('');
      setSelectedIndex(0);
      setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 100);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (!query) return [];
    
    // Command mode
    if (query.startsWith('>')) {
       const cmdQuery = query.slice(1).trim().toLowerCase();
       return SUGGESTED_COMMANDS.filter(c => c.label.toLowerCase().includes(cmdQuery) || c.cmd.toLowerCase().includes(cmdQuery))
        .map(c => ({ type: 'command', id: c.cmd, label: c.label, icon: c.icon, desc: c.cmd }));
    }

    // Navigation mode
    return allNavItems
      .filter(i => i.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()) || i.desc.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()))
      .map(i => ({ type: 'nav', id: i.id, label: i.label, icon: i.icon, desc: i.desc }));
  }, [query, allNavItems]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault();
        // Toggle handled by parent
      }
      
      if (!isOpen) return;

      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        onClose();
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSelectedIndex(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, filteredItems.length - 1));
      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSelectedIndex(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 0));
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && filteredItems.length > 0) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const item = filteredItems[selectedIndex];
        if (item) {
          if (item.type === 'nav') {
            onNavigate(item.id);
          } else if (item.type === 'command') {
            const navId = item.id.replace('> ', '');
            onNavigate(navId);
          }
          onClose();
        }
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handler);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, [isOpen, onClose, filteredItems, selectedIndex, onNavigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedIndex(0);
  }, [query]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-start justify-center pt-[12vh]" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-at-bg/80 backdrop-blur-sm" />
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-2xl bg-at-elevated border border-at-border rounded-[8px] shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-fade-in-up flex flex-col"
        onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Search input header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-4 border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface">
          <Search size={20} className="text-at-muted flex-shrink-0" />
          <input
            ref={inputRef}
            value={query}
            onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search resources, IP addresses, or type > for commands..."
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-[15px] font-medium text-at-text outline-none placeholder:text-at-disabled"
          />
          {query && (
            <button onClick={() => setQuery('')} className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-at-subtle text-at-muted mr-2">
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          )}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
             <kbd className="text-[10px] bg-at-bg border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-at-muted">ESC</kbd>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Results area */}
        <div className="max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar flex-1">
          {query && filteredItems.length > 0 && (
            <div className="p-2">
              <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-[0.05em]">
                {query.startsWith('>') ? 'Commands' : 'Navigation & Pages'}
              </div>
              {filteredItems.map((item, idx) => {
                const selected = idx === selectedIndex;
                const Icon = item.icon;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={item.id}
                    onMouseEnter={() => setSelectedIndex(idx)}
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (item.type === 'nav') onNavigate(item.id);
                      else onNavigate(item.id.replace('> ', ''));
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-[6px] text-left transition-colors ${
                      selected ? 'bg-at-subtle border border-at-border-hover' : 'border border-transparent hover:bg-white/[0.02]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className={`p-1.5 rounded ${selected ? 'bg-at-accent-muted text-at-accent' : 'bg-at-bg text-at-muted'}`}>
                       <Icon size={16} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <div className={`text-[13px] ${selected ? 'text-at-text font-medium' : 'text-at-text-secondary'}`}>{item.label}</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted mt-0.5">{item.desc}</div>
                    </div>
                    <ArrowRight size={14} className={selected ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-transparent'} />
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          {!query && (
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row min-h-[300px]">
              {/* Left column: Recent & Suggestions */}
              <div className="flex-1 p-2 border-b md:border-b-0 md:border-r border-at-border">
                <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-[0.05em]">Recent Searches</div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  {RECENT_SEARCHES.map(s => (
                    <button
                      key={s}
                      onClick={() => setQuery(s)}
                      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-[6px] hover:bg-white/[0.03] text-left group transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Clock size={14} className="text-at-disabled group-hover:text-at-muted" />
                      <span className="text-[13px] font-mono text-at-muted group-hover:text-at-text-secondary">{s}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <div className="px-3 py-2 mt-4 text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-[0.05em]">Commands</div>
                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                  {SUGGESTED_COMMANDS.map(c => (
                    <button
                      key={c.cmd}
                      onClick={() => setQuery(c.cmd)}
                      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-[6px] hover:bg-white/[0.03] text-left group transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Command size={14} className="text-at-disabled group-hover:text-at-muted" />
                      <span className="text-[13px] text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text">{c.label}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Right column: Filters & Types */}
              <div className="flex-1 p-2 bg-at-subtle/50">
                <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-[0.05em]">Search by Entity</div>
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 px-1">
                  {ENTITY_TYPES.map(et => {
                    const EtIcon = et.icon;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={et.label}
                        className="flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 rounded-[6px] hover:bg-white/[0.04] text-left group transition-colors border border-transparent hover:border-at-border"
                        onClick={() => setQuery(et.example)}
                      >
                        <div className="p-1 rounded bg-at-bg border border-at-border text-at-muted group-hover:text-at-accent group-hover:border-at-accent/30 transition-colors">
                          <EtIcon size={12} />
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[12px] text-at-text-secondary flex-1">{et.label}</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-disabled group-hover:text-at-muted">{et.example}</span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                <div className="px-3 py-2 mt-4 text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-[0.05em]">Smart Filters</div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 px-3 pt-1">
                  {['severity:critical', 'country:russia', 'type:ipv4', 'last_seen:24h'].map(f => (
                    <button key={f} className="at-chip text-[11px] hover:border-at-accent/50 hover:text-at-accent transition-colors bg-at-bg" onClick={() => setQuery(f)}>
                      {f}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {query && filteredItems.length === 0 && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-center h-[300px]">
              <Search size={32} className="text-at-disabled mb-4" />
              <div className="text-[15px] font-medium text-at-text mb-1">No results found</div>
              <div className="text-[13px] text-at-muted">
                We couldn't find anything matching "<span className="text-at-text-secondary font-mono">{query}</span>"
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer hints */}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><kbd className="bg-at-bg border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-at-text-secondary">â†‘</kbd><kbd className="bg-at-bg border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-at-text-secondary -ml-1">â†“</kbd> to navigate</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><kbd className="bg-at-bg border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-at-text-secondary">â†µ</kbd> to select</span>
          </div>

             <kbd className="bg-at-bg border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono text-at-text-secondary">&gt;</kbd> for commands
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface HeaderProps {

export default function Header({ onOpenSearch }: HeaderProps) {

    return () => clearInterval(t);

  return (
    <header className="h-[52px] flex-shrink-0 flex items-center justify-between px-4 border-b border-at-border bg-at-bg/95 backdrop-blur-md z-40 relative">

import { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Search, Filter, X, LayoutGrid, TerminalSquare } from 'lucide-react';
import PageHeader from './PageHeader';

interface IntelLayoutProps {
  breadcrumbs: { label: string }[];
  title: string;
  description: string;
  headerActions?: ReactNode;
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  searchValue: string;
  onSearchChange: (val: string) => void;
  activeFilters: string[];
  onRemoveFilter: (filter: string) => void;
  resultsCount: number;
  tableContent: ReactNode;
  inspectorContent?: ReactNode;
  isInspectorOpen: boolean;
  onCloseInspector: () => void;
  advancedMode?: boolean;
  onToggleAdvanced?: () => void;
}

export default function IntelLayout({
  breadcrumbs, title, description, headerActions,
  searchPlaceholder = 'Search...', searchValue, onSearchChange,
  activeFilters, onRemoveFilter, resultsCount,
  tableContent, inspectorContent, isInspectorOpen, onCloseInspector,
  advancedMode = false, onToggleAdvanced
}: IntelLayoutProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 bg-[#060606]">
      <PageHeader
        breadcrumbs={breadcrumbs}
        title={title}
        description={description}
        actions={headerActions}
      />
      
      {/* Query Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col border-b border-at-border bg-at-bg">
        <div className="p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          
          {/* Search Input */}
          <div className="flex-1 max-w-2xl flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className={`flex-1 relative flex items-center ${advancedMode ? 'border-at-accent/50' : 'border-at-border'} border rounded bg-at-surface transition-colors focus-within:border-at-accent`}>
              <div className="pl-3 text-at-disabled">
                {advancedMode ? <TerminalSquare size={14} className="text-at-accent" /> : <Search size={14} />}
              </div>
              <input
                value={searchValue}
                onChange={e => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
                placeholder={advancedMode ? "e.g. severity:critical AND type:domain" : searchPlaceholder}
                className={`w-full bg-transparent outline-none px-2 py-2 text-[12px] ${advancedMode ? 'font-mono text-at-text' : 'text-at-text-secondary'}`}
              />
              {searchValue && (
                <button onClick={() => onSearchChange('')} className="pr-3 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text transition-colors">
                  <X size={14} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            {onToggleAdvanced && (
              <button 
                onClick={onToggleAdvanced} 
                className={`p-2 border rounded transition-colors ${advancedMode ? 'bg-at-accent/10 border-at-accent/30 text-at-accent' : 'bg-at-surface border-at-border text-at-muted hover:text-at-text'}`}
                title={advancedMode ? "Switch to standard search" : "Switch to advanced query"}
              >
                <LayoutGrid size={14} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button className="at-btn at-btn-ghost at-btn-sm text-at-muted hover:text-at-text"><Filter size={14} /> Filters</button>
            <div className="h-4 w-px bg-at-border mx-1"></div>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted">{resultsCount.toLocaleString()} RESULTS</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Filter Chips */}
        {activeFilters.length > 0 && (
          <div className="px-4 pb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mr-1">Active:</span>
            {activeFilters.map(f => (
              <span key={f} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-at-border bg-at-surface text-at-text-secondary">
                {f}
                <button onClick={() => onRemoveFilter(f)} className="text-at-disabled hover:text-at-accent transition-colors"><X size={10} /></button>
              </span>
            ))}
            <button className="text-[10px] text-at-accent hover:underline ml-2" onClick={() => activeFilters.forEach(f => onRemoveFilter(f))}>Clear All</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Main Workspace (Table + Inspector) */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 min-h-0 relative">
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto bg-[#0a0a0a]">
          {tableContent}
        </div>
        
        {/* Right Inspector */}
        {isInspectorOpen && (
          <div className="w-[360px] shrink-0 border-l border-at-border bg-at-bg shadow-[-8px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)] flex flex-col animate-slide-left z-20">
            {inspectorContent}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ChevronRight, Calendar } from 'lucide-react';

interface BreadcrumbItem {
  label: string;
  id?: string; // If provided, could be used for navigation
}

interface PageHeaderProps {
  breadcrumbs: BreadcrumbItem[];
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  timeRange?: string;
  actions?: ReactNode;
  filters?: ReactNode;
}

export default function PageHeader({
  breadcrumbs,
  title,
  description,
  timeRange,
  actions,
  filters
}: PageHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-b border-at-border bg-at-bg shrink-0">
      
      {/* Top Row: Breadcrumbs & Time Range */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <nav className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wide">
          {breadcrumbs.map((crumb, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {idx > 0 && <ChevronRight size={12} className="opacity-50" />}
              <span className={idx === breadcrumbs.length - 1 ? 'text-at-muted' : ''}>
                {crumb.label}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </nav>
        
        {timeRange && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted bg-at-surface px-2 py-1 rounded border border-at-border">
            <Calendar size={11} className="text-at-disabled" />
            {timeRange}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Middle Row: Title/Desc & Actions */}
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[20px] font-semibold text-at-text tracking-tight leading-tight">
            {title}
          </h1>
          {description && (
            <p className="text-[13px] text-at-muted mt-1 leading-snug">
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        
        {actions && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
            {actions}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Row: Filters (Optional) */}
      {filters && (
        <div className="mt-1 pt-3 border-t border-at-border/50 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          {filters}
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

File: C:\Users\rishi\ANTI AI\ANTI-TODE\Xyberah-redesign\src\components\layout\Sidebar.tsx
==================================================
import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Search, Star, PanelLeftClose, PanelLeft, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react';
import { TOP_LEVEL_ITEMS, NAV_GROUPS, UTILITY_ITEMS, BOTTOM_ITEMS, type NavItem, type NavGroup } from '../../data/navigation';

interface SidebarProps {
  activeView: string;
  onNavigate: (id: string) => void;
  collapsed: boolean;
  onToggleCollapse: () => void;
}

export default function Sidebar({ activeView, onNavigate, collapsed, onToggleCollapse }: SidebarProps) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [collapsedGroups, setCollapsedGroups] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [favorites, setFavorites] = useState<string[]>(() => {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('at_favorites') || '[]'); } catch { return []; }
  });

  const toggleGroup = (title: string) => {
    setCollapsedGroups(prev => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.has(title) ? next.delete(title) : next.add(title);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const toggleFavorite = (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    setFavorites(prev => {
      const next = prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter(f => f !== id) : [...prev, id];
      localStorage.setItem('at_favorites', JSON.stringify(next));
      return next;
    });
  };

  const displayGroups = useMemo(() => {
    let groups: NavGroup[] = [...NAV_GROUPS];
    if (search) {
      const q = search.toLowerCase();
      const matched = NAV_GROUPS.flatMap(g => g.items).filter(i =>
        i.label.toLowerCase().includes(q) || i.desc.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
      return [{ title: 'Results', items: matched }];
    }
    if (favorites.length > 0) {
      const favItems = [...TOP_LEVEL_ITEMS, ...NAV_GROUPS.flatMap(g => g.items), ...UTILITY_ITEMS, ...BOTTOM_ITEMS].filter(i => favorites.includes(i.id));
      if (favItems.length > 0) groups.unshift({ title: 'Favorites', items: favItems });
    }
    return groups;
  }, [search, favorites]);

  const renderItem = (item: NavItem) => {
    const isActive = activeView === item.id;
    const isFav = favorites.includes(item.id);
    return (
      <a
        href={`#${item.id}`}
        key={item.id}
        onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onNavigate(item.id); }}
        className={`group w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors relative cursor-pointer ${
          collapsed ? 'justify-center px-2 py-2.5' : 'px-3 py-2'
        } ${
          isActive
            ? 'bg-at-surface text-at-text'
            : 'text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary hover:bg-white/[0.03]'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Tooltip for collapsed mode */}
        {collapsed && (
          <div className="absolute left-full ml-2 px-2 py-1 bg-at-elevated border border-at-border text-at-text-secondary text-xs rounded opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 pointer-events-none transition-opacity z-50 whitespace-nowrap shadow-lg">
            {item.label}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Active indicator rail - thin red left rail */}
        {isActive && (
          <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-[2px] bg-at-accent" />
        )}

        <item.icon size={collapsed ? 18 : 16} className={isActive ? 'text-at-accent' : ''} />

        {!collapsed && (
          <>
            <span className={`flex-1 text-left text-[13px] truncate ${isActive ? 'font-medium' : ''}`}>
              {item.label}
            </span>
            <button
              onClick={(e) => toggleFavorite(item.id, e)}
              className={`p-0.5 rounded transition-opacity ${
                isFav ? 'text-amber-400 opacity-100' : 'opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-60 text-at-muted hover:text-amber-400'
              }`}
            >
              <Star size={11} fill={isFav ? 'currentColor' : 'none'} />
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </a>
    );
  };

  return (
    <aside
      className={`flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col h-full border-r border-at-border bg-at-bg transition-all duration-300 z-30 ${
        collapsed ? 'w-[56px]' : 'w-[250px]'
      }`}
    >
      {/* Search / Collapse toggle */}
      <div className={`flex items-center border-b border-at-border flex-shrink-0 ${collapsed ? 'justify-center py-2' : 'gap-2 px-3 py-2'}`}>
        {collapsed ? (
          <button onClick={onToggleCollapse} className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-at-muted hover:text-at-text transition-colors">
            <PanelLeft size={16} />
          </button>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search size={13} className="absolute left-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-at-disabled" />
              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Find..."
                className="at-input w-full pl-7 pr-2 py-1.5 text-xs bg-at-subtle"
              />
            </div>
            <button onClick={onToggleCollapse} className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-at-muted hover:text-at-text transition-colors flex-shrink-0">
              <PanelLeftClose size={16} />
            </button>
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Navigation container */}
      <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden py-2 flex flex-col gap-0.5" style={{ scrollbarGutter: 'stable' }}>
        
        {/* Top Level Items */}
        {!search && (
          <div className="mb-2">
            {TOP_LEVEL_ITEMS.map(renderItem)}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Groups */}
        {displayGroups.map(group => {
          const isGroupCollapsed = collapsedGroups.has(group.title) && !search;
          return (
            <div key={group.title} className="mb-0.5">
              {!collapsed && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => toggleGroup(group.title)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 pt-3 pb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-at-disabled hover:text-at-muted transition-colors"
                >
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    {group.title === 'Favorites' && <Star size={9} fill="currentColor" className="text-amber-400" />}
                    {group.title}
                  </span>
                  {!search && (isGroupCollapsed ? <ChevronRight size={11} /> : <ChevronDown size={11} />)}
                </button>
              )}
              {collapsed && <div className="h-px bg-at-border mx-2 my-2" />}
              <div className={`overflow-hidden transition-all duration-200 ${isGroupCollapsed && !search ? 'max-h-0' : 'max-h-[2000px]'}`}>
                {group.items.map(renderItem)}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}

        {/* Spacer to push utilities down if needed, but usually we just list them */}
        {!search && (
          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-at-border mx-2">
            {UTILITY_ITEMS.map(renderItem)}
          </div>
        )}
      </nav>

      {/* Settings at the absolute bottom */}
      {!search && (
        <div className="border-t border-at-border flex-shrink-0 py-1">
          {BOTTOM_ITEMS.map(renderItem)}
        </div>
      )}
    </aside>
  );
}

const countries = [

  'rm -rf /var/log/*', 'cat /proc/cpuinfo', 'echo "*/5 * * * * /tmp/.x" >> /var/spool/cron/crontabs/root',
];
const usernames = ['root', 'admin', 'test', 'user', 'oracle', 'postgres', 'ubuntu', 'pi', 'guest', 'support'];
const signatures = [
  'Log4j RCE', 'Reverse Shell', 'Crypto-Miner', 'Web Shell', 'Cobalt Strike Beacon',
  'SSH Brute Force', 'SQL Injection', 'Directory Traversal', 'XSS Payload', 'SSRF Attempt',
];

function randomIp(): string {
  return `${Math.floor(Math.random() * 223) + 1}.${Math.floor(Math.random() * 256)}.${Math.floor(Math.random() * 256)}.${Math.floor(Math.random() * 256)}`;
}

function randomFrom<T>(arr: T[]): T {
  return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
}

function randomDate(daysBack: number): string {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - Math.floor(Math.random() * daysBack));
  d.setHours(Math.floor(Math.random() * 24), Math.floor(Math.random() * 60));
  return d.toISOString();
}

export function generateMockEvents(count: number): MockThreatEvent[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => {
    const c = randomFrom(countries);
    return {
      id: `evt-${i}`,
      timestamp: randomDate(7),
      srcIp: randomIp(),
      dstPort: randomFrom([22, 23, 80, 443, 445, 2222, 3306, 3389, 5900, 8080, 8443]),
      honeypot: randomFrom(honeypots),
      eventType: randomFrom(eventTypes),
      country: c.name,
      countryCode: c.code,
      severity: randomFrom(['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'] as const),
      command: Math.random() > 0.4 ? randomFrom(commands) : undefined,
      username: Math.random() > 0.3 ? randomFrom(usernames) : undefined,
      asn: `AS${Math.floor(Math.random() * 60000) + 1000}`,
    };
  }).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timestamp).getTime() - new Date(a.timestamp).getTime());
}

export function generateMockHosts(count: number): MockHost[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => {
    const c = randomFrom(countries);
    const score = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);
    return {
      ip: randomIp(),
      country: c.name,
      countryCode: c.code,
      totalScore: score,
      riskLevel: score >= 80 ? 'CRITICAL' : score >= 60 ? 'HIGH' : score >= 30 ? 'MEDIUM' : 'LOW',
      eventCount: Math.floor(Math.random() * 500) + 1,
      firstSeen: randomDate(30),
      lastSeen: randomDate(2),
      asn: `AS${Math.floor(Math.random() * 60000) + 1000}`,
      org: randomFrom(['DigitalOcean', 'Amazon AWS', 'Hetzner', 'OVH', 'Alibaba Cloud', 'Tencent', 'Linode', 'Vultr']),
      ports: Array.from({ length: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) + 1 }, () => randomFrom([22, 23, 80, 443, 445, 3389])),
      signatures: Array.from({ length: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) }, () => randomFrom(signatures)),
      isBlacklisted: Math.random() > 0.6,
    };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.totalScore - a.totalScore);
}

export function generateMockCves(count: number): MockCve[] {
  const vendors = ['Apache', 'Microsoft', 'Google', 'Oracle', 'Linux', 'Cisco', 'VMware', 'Fortinet', 'Palo Alto', 'Adobe'];
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => {
    const cvss = +(Math.random() * 10).toFixed(1);
    return {
      id: `CVE-2024-${String(i + 1000).padStart(5, '0')}`,
      description: randomFrom([
        'Remote code execution via deserialization vulnerability.',
        'Privilege escalation through improper access control.',
        'SQL injection in authentication endpoint.',
        'Buffer overflow in network stack.',
        'Cross-site scripting in admin panel.',
        'Authentication bypass via crafted token.',
        'Path traversal allowing arbitrary file read.',
        'Denial of service through malformed packet.',
      ]),
      severity: cvss >= 9 ? 'critical' : cvss >= 7 ? 'high' : cvss >= 4 ? 'medium' : 'low',
      cvss,
      published: randomDate(90),
      vendor: randomFrom(vendors),
      product: randomFrom(['Server', 'Firewall', 'Database', 'Framework', 'Runtime', 'SDK', 'API Gateway']),
      exploitAvailable: Math.random() > 0.7,
    };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.cvss - a.cvss);
}

export function generateMockIocs(count: number): MockIoc[] {
  const sources = ['abuse.ch', 'AlienVault OTX', 'ThreatFox', 'URLhaus', 'FeodoTracker', 'MalwareBazaar', 'IPsum', 'BlocklistDE'];
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => {
    const type = randomFrom(['ip', 'domain', 'hash', 'url'] as const);
    let value = '';
    switch (type) {
      case 'ip': value = randomIp(); break;
      case 'domain': value = `${randomFrom(['evil', 'malware', 'c2', 'botnet', 'phish'])}.${randomFrom(['ru', 'cn', 'xyz', 'top', 'cc', 'tk'])}`;  break;
      case 'hash': value = Array.from({ length: 64 }, () => '0123456789abcdef'[Math.floor(Math.random() * 16)]).join(''); break;
      case 'url': value = `http://${randomIp()}:${randomFrom([8080, 443, 80])}/${randomFrom(['payload', 'bot', 'loader', 'dropper'])}.${randomFrom(['exe', 'sh', 'py', 'bin'])}`; break;
    }
    return {
      id: `ioc-${i}`,
      type,
      value,
      source: randomFrom(sources),
      severity: randomFrom(['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'] as const),
      firstSeen: randomDate(60),
      lastSeen: randomDate(5),
      tags: Array.from({ length: Math.floor(Math.random() * 3) + 1 }, () => randomFrom(['malware', 'botnet', 'c2', 'phishing', 'ransomware', 'trojan', 'apt', 'miner'])),
    };
  });
}

  const titles = [
    'Critical Zero-Day Vulnerability Found in Enterprise VPN Appliance',
    'New Ransomware Gang Targets Healthcare Sector with Double Extortion',
    'APT Group Deploys Novel Backdoor in Supply Chain Attack',

    'Critical Vulnerability in Apache Struts Under Active Exploitation',
    'State-Sponsored Actors Target Critical Infrastructure in Europe',
    'New Stealer Malware Sold on Dark Web Forums for $200/month',
    'Russian APT Group Exploits Microsoft Exchange Vulnerabilities',
    'FBI Seizes Infrastructure of Notorious Ransomware Group',
  ];
  const sources = ['BleepingComputer', 'The Hacker News', 'Krebs on Security', 'Dark Reading', 'SecurityWeek', 'The Record', 'CISA', 'Mandiant'];
  const categories = ['Vulnerability', 'Ransomware', 'APT', 'Malware', 'Data Breach', 'Advisory'];
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({
    id: `news-${i}`,
    title: titles[i % titles.length],
    source: randomFrom(sources),
    published: randomDate(14),
    category: randomFrom(categories),
    url: '#',
  })).sort((a, b) => new Date(b.published).getTime() - new Date(a.published).getTime());
}

export function generateMockActors(count: number): MockActor[] {
  const actors = [
    { name: 'APT28', aliases: ['Fancy Bear', 'Sofacy'], country: 'Russia', motivation: 'Espionage' },

    { name: 'Lazarus Group', aliases: ['Hidden Cobra', 'Zinc'], country: 'North Korea', motivation: 'Financial/Espionage' },

    { name: 'Equation Group', aliases: ['EQGRP'], country: 'United States', motivation: 'Espionage' },
    { name: 'MuddyWater', aliases: ['Mercury', 'SeedWorm'], country: 'Iran', motivation: 'Espionage' },
    { name: 'Kimsuky', aliases: ['Velvet Chollima'], country: 'North Korea', motivation: 'Espionage' },
  ];
  return actors.slice(0, count).map((a, i) => ({
    id: `actor-${i}`,
    ...a,
    firstSeen: randomDate(365 * 5),
    lastSeen: randomDate(30),
    ttps: Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => `T${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1600) + 1}`),
    targets: Array.from({ length: 3 }, () => randomFrom(['Government', 'Defense', 'Finance', 'Energy', 'Healthcare', 'Technology', 'Telecom'])),
  }));
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Download, Users, Crosshair, Map, ShieldAlert, Activity, FileText, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { MOCK_ACTORS, type MockActor } from '../data/mockData';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

export default function ActorsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [advancedMode, setAdvancedMode] = useState(false);
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedActorId, setSelectedActorId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState('Profile');

  const selectedActor = useMemo(() => MOCK_ACTORS.find(a => a.id === selectedActorId) || null, [selectedActorId]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_ACTORS.filter(actor => {
      const matchSearch = !search || 
        actor.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
        actor.aliases.some(a => a.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
      return matchSearch;
    });
  }, [search, activeFilters]);

  const removeFilter = (f: string) => setActiveFilters(prev => prev.filter(x => x !== f));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Designation</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Aliases</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Origin</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Motivation</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Targets</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(actor => {
          const isSelected = selectedActorId === actor.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={actor.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedActorId(actor.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <div className={`w-6 h-6 rounded flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/20 text-at-accent' : 'bg-at-surface border border-at-border text-at-muted group-hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}>
                    <Users size={12} />
                  </div>
                  <span className={`font-mono text-[13px] font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>{actor.name}</span>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-at-muted">
                {actor.aliases.join(', ')}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary">
                  <Map size={12} className="text-at-disabled" /> {actor.country}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-at-muted">{actor.motivation}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex gap-1 flex-wrap max-w-[200px]">
                  {actor.targets.slice(0, 2).map(t => <span key={t} className="at-chip text-[9px] bg-at-bg border-transparent">{t}</span>)}
                  {actor.targets.length > 2 && <span className="at-chip text-[9px] bg-at-bg border-transparent">+{actor.targets.length - 2}</span>}
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  const inspector = selectedActor ? (
    <>
      <div className="p-5 border-b border-at-border relative bg-gradient-to-br from-at-surface to-transparent">
        <button onClick={() => setSelectedActorId(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text"><X size={14} /></button>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-4">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded border border-at-accent bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(214,40,40,0.15)]">
            <Users size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="pr-4 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Threat Actor</div>
            <div className="text-[16px] font-mono font-bold text-at-text truncate">{selectedActor.name}</div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted mt-1">{selectedActor.aliases.join(' â€¢ ')}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="at-badge at-badge-high">{selectedActor.motivation.toUpperCase()}</span>
          <span className="at-badge bg-at-surface text-at-text-secondary border-at-border flex items-center gap-1"><Map size={10}/> {selectedActor.country}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex border-b border-at-border px-2 shrink-0">
        {['Profile', 'TTPs', 'Campaigns'].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab}
            onClick={() => setInspectorTab(tab)}
            className={`px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${inspectorTab === tab ? 'border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'border-transparent text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}
          >
            {tab}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-6">
        {inspectorTab === 'Profile' && (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">First Tracked</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{selectedActor.firstSeen.split('T')[0]}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">Last Active</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{selectedActor.lastSeen.split('T')[0]}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-5">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Crosshair size={12}/> Targeted Sectors</div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {selectedActor.targets.map(t => (
                  <span key={t} className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border text-at-text-secondary">{t}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-5">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Activity size={12}/> Known Tooling</div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {['Cobalt Strike', 'Mimikatz', 'Custom Dropper', 'Powershell Empire'].map(t => (
                  <span key={t} className="at-chip text-[10px] font-mono bg-[#060606] border-at-border text-at-muted">{t}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        
        {inspectorTab === 'TTPs' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted mb-2">Mapped to MITRE ATT&CK Framework</div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {selectedActor.ttps.map(ttp => (
                <div key={ttp} className="p-2 border border-at-border rounded bg-at-surface flex items-center justify-between group cursor-pointer hover:border-at-muted transition-colors">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-accent">{ttp}</span>
                  <ChevronRight size={12} className="text-at-disabled group-hover:text-at-text" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50">
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary w-full text-[11px] justify-center"><FileText size={12} /> View Full Dossier</button>
      </div>
    </>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <IntelLayout
      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Intelligence' }, { label: 'Actors' }]}
      title="Threat Actors"
      description="Dossiers and profiles on state-sponsored, financial, and hacktivist groups."
      headerActions={
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> Export MISP</button>
      }
      searchValue={search}
      onSearchChange={setSearch}
      searchPlaceholder="Search actor name, aliases, country..."
      activeFilters={activeFilters}
      onRemoveFilter={removeFilter}
      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedActorId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedActorId(null)}
      advancedMode={advancedMode}
      onToggleAdvanced={() => setAdvancedMode(!advancedMode)}
    />
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Download, ShieldAlert, Target, AlertTriangle, ExternalLink, Calendar, Server, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { MOCK_CVES, type MockCve } from '../data/mockData';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

export default function CvePage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [advancedMode, setAdvancedMode] = useState(false);
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>(['has_exploit:true']);
  const [selectedCveId, setSelectedCveId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState('Overview');

  const selectedCve = useMemo(() => MOCK_CVES.find(c => c.id === selectedCveId) || null, [selectedCveId]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_CVES.filter(cve => {
      const matchSearch = !search || 
        cve.id.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || 
        cve.description.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      const matchExploit = activeFilters.includes('has_exploit:true') ? cve.exploitAvailable : true;
      return matchSearch && matchExploit;
    });
  }, [search, activeFilters]);

  const removeFilter = (f: string) => setActiveFilters(prev => prev.filter(x => x !== f));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-24">CVE ID</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">CVSS</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Severity</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-1/3">Description</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Product</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Published</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(cve => {
          const isSelected = selectedCveId === cve.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={cve.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedCveId(cve.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {cve.exploitAvailable && <Target size={12} className="text-at-accent shrink-0" title="Exploit Available" />}
                  <span className={`font-mono text-[12px] font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>{cve.id}</span>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`font-mono text-[12px] ${cve.cvss >= 9 ? 'text-at-accent' : cve.cvss >= 7 ? 'text-amber-500' : 'text-at-muted'}`}>{cve.cvss.toFixed(1)}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`at-badge ${cve.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : cve.severity === 'high' ? 'at-badge-high' : 'at-badge-medium'}`}>
                  {cve.severity}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-at-muted">
                <div className="line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">{cve.description}</div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary">
                {cve.vendor} {cve.product}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted">
                {cve.published.split('T')[0]}
              </td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  const inspector = selectedCve ? (
    <>
      <div className="p-5 border-b border-at-border relative">
        <button onClick={() => setSelectedCveId(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text"><X size={14} /></button>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-4">
          <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${selectedCve.severity === 'critical' ? 'border-at-accent bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(214,40,40,0.15)]' : 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500'}`}>
            <ShieldAlert size={24} />
          </div>
          <div className="pr-4 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">Vulnerability</div>
            <div className="text-[16px] font-mono font-bold text-at-text truncate">{selectedCve.id}</div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted mt-1">{selectedCve.vendor} {selectedCve.product}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`at-badge ${selectedCve.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : 'at-badge-high'}`}>CVSS {selectedCve.cvss.toFixed(1)}</span>
          {selectedCve.exploitAvailable && <span className="at-badge bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent border-at-accent/30 flex items-center gap-1"><Target size={10}/> EXPLOITED IN WILD</span>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex border-b border-at-border px-2 shrink-0">
        {['Overview', 'Affected Systems', 'References'].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab}
            onClick={() => setInspectorTab(tab)}
            className={`px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${inspectorTab === tab ? 'border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'border-transparent text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}
          >
            {tab}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-6">
        {inspectorTab === 'Overview' && (
          <>
            <div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-2 uppercase tracking-widest font-semibold">Description</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-at-text-secondary leading-relaxed bg-at-surface/50 p-3 rounded border border-at-border">
                {selectedCve.description}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Calendar size={12}/> Published</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{selectedCve.published.split('T')[0]}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        
        {inspectorTab === 'Affected Systems' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-32 text-center text-at-muted">
            <Server size={24} className="mb-2 opacity-30" />
            <div className="text-[11px]">No internally affected systems found for this CVE.</div>
            <button className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-at-accent hover:underline">Run Network Scan</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50 space-y-2">
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary w-full text-[11px] justify-center flex items-center gap-2">View NVD Record <ExternalLink size={12} /></button>
      </div>
    </>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <IntelLayout
      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Intelligence' }, { label: 'Vulnerabilities' }]}
      title="CVE Database"
      description="Track and analyze Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures."
      headerActions={
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> Export JSON</button>
      }
      searchValue={search}
      onSearchChange={setSearch}
      searchPlaceholder="Search CVE-ID, vendor, or description..."
      activeFilters={activeFilters}
      onRemoveFilter={removeFilter}
      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedCveId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedCveId(null)}
      advancedMode={advancedMode}
      onToggleAdvanced={() => setAdvancedMode(!advancedMode)}
    />
  );
}

                      <th className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Honeypot</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Severity</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {recentHoneypot.map(e => (
                      <tr key={e.id} className="border-b border-at-border/50 hover:bg-white/[0.02] transition-colors cursor-pointer group">

                          <span className="at-entity text-[11px] group-hover:text-at-accent transition-colors">{e.srcIp}</span>

                          <span className={`at-badge ${e.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : e.severity === 'high' ? 'at-badge-high' : e.severity === 'medium' ? 'at-badge-medium' : 'at-badge-low'}`}>
                            {e.severity}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>

          {/* SECONDARY COLUMN */}
          <div className="space-y-6 flex flex-col">

                  Attack Geography

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1.5">
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text transition-colors">{country}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-muted">{count}</span>

                      <div className="h-full bg-at-text-secondary rounded-full transition-all group-hover:bg-at-text" style={{ width: `${(count / maxCountry) * 100}%` }} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Top ASNs */}
            <div className="at-card flex flex-col">
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-at-border">
                <h3 className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-at-text uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Network size={13} />

import {
  Search, Filter, RefreshCw, Download, ChevronDown, X,
  Terminal, Globe, Server, Clock, Activity, AlertTriangle,
  ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { MOCK_EVENTS, type MockThreatEvent } from '../data/mockData';
import PageHeader from '../components/layout/PageHeader';

interface HoneypotLogsProps {
  onNavigate: (id: string) => void;
}

const SEVERITY_CONFIG = {
  critical: { badge: 'at-badge-critical', label: 'Critical' },
  high: { badge: 'at-badge-high', label: 'High' },
  medium: { badge: 'at-badge-medium', label: 'Medium' },
  low: { badge: 'at-badge-low', label: 'Low' },
};

export default function HoneypotLogsPage({ onNavigate }: HoneypotLogsProps) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [sevFilter, setSevFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [honeypotFilter, setHoneypotFilter] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState<MockThreatEvent | null>(null);

  const uniqueHoneypots = useMemo(() => [...new Set(MOCK_EVENTS.map(e => e.honeypot))].sort(), []);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_EVENTS.filter(e => {
      const matchSearch = !search || e.srcIp.includes(search) || e.eventType.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || (e.command?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));
      const matchSev = sevFilter === 'ALL' || e.severity === sevFilter;
      const matchHp = honeypotFilter === 'ALL' || e.honeypot === honeypotFilter;
      return matchSearch && matchSev && matchHp;
    });
  }, [search, sevFilter, honeypotFilter]);

  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
    total: filtered.length,
    critical: filtered.filter(e => e.severity === 'critical').length,
    high: filtered.filter(e => e.severity === 'high').length,
    unique: new Set(filtered.map(e => e.srcIp)).size,
  }), [filtered]);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 min-h-0 bg-at-bg">
      {/* Main content */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 border-r border-at-border">
        <PageHeader
          breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Operations' }, { label: 'Honeypot Logs' }]}
          title="Honeypot Logs"
          description={`${stats.total.toLocaleString()} events Â· ${stats.unique} unique sources`}
          actions={
            <>
              <button className="at-btn at-btn-ghost at-btn-sm"><RefreshCw size={13} /> Refresh</button>
              <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> Export</button>
            </>
          }
          filters={
            <>
              <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-xs">
                <Search size={14} className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-at-disabled" />
                <input
                  value={search}
                  onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Search IPs, commands, types..."
                  className="at-input w-full pl-8 text-xs bg-at-subtle"
                />
              </div>

              <select value={sevFilter} onChange={e => setSevFilter(e.target.value)} className="at-input text-xs py-1.5 bg-at-subtle">
                <option value="ALL">All Severities</option>
                <option value="critical">Critical</option>
                <option value="high">High</option>
                <option value="medium">Medium</option>
                <option value="low">Low</option>
              </select>

              <select value={honeypotFilter} onChange={e => setHoneypotFilter(e.target.value)} className="at-input text-xs py-1.5 bg-at-subtle">
                <option value="ALL">All Honeypots</option>
                {uniqueHoneypots.map(h => <option key={h} value={h}>{h}</option>)}
              </select>

              {(sevFilter !== 'ALL' || honeypotFilter !== 'ALL' || search) && (
                <button onClick={() => { setSearch(''); setSevFilter('ALL'); setHoneypotFilter('ALL'); }} className="at-btn at-btn-ghost at-btn-sm text-at-accent hover:bg-at-accent/10">
                  <X size={12} /> Clear
                </button>
              )}

              {/* Mini stat chips */}
              <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="at-chip bg-at-bg"><AlertTriangle size={11} className="text-sev-critical" /> {stats.critical} Critical</span>
                <span className="at-chip bg-at-bg"><Activity size={11} className="text-sev-high" /> {stats.high} High</span>
              </div>
            </>
          }
        />

        {/* Table */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <table className="at-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Timestamp</th>
                <th>Source IP</th>
                <th>Port</th>
                <th>Type</th>
                <th>Honeypot</th>
                <th>Country</th>
                <th>Username</th>
                <th>Severity</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filtered.map(e => {
                const sev = SEVERITY_CONFIG[e.severity];
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={e.id}
                    className={`cursor-pointer ${selectedEvent?.id === e.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedEvent(e)}
                  >
                    <td className="font-mono text-xs text-at-muted whitespace-nowrap">
                      {new Date(e.timestamp).toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'short', day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit', hour12: false })}
                    </td>
                    <td><span className="at-entity">{e.srcIp}</span></td>
                    <td className="font-mono text-xs">{e.dstPort}</td>
                    <td className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{e.eventType.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</td>
                    <td className="text-xs text-at-muted">{e.honeypot}</td>
                    <td className="text-xs">{e.country}</td>
                    <td className="font-mono text-xs text-at-muted">{e.username || 'â€”'}</td>
                    <td><span className={`at-badge ${sev.badge}`}>{sev.label}</span></td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detail inspector panel */}
      {selectedEvent && (
        <div className="w-[360px] flex-shrink-0 border-l border-at-border bg-at-bg overflow-y-auto animate-slide-in-right">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-at-border flex items-center justify-between">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-at-text">Event Detail</h3>
            <button onClick={() => setSelectedEvent(null)} className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-white/5 text-at-muted"><X size={16} /></button>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 space-y-4">
            {/* Severity */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className={`at-badge ${SEVERITY_CONFIG[selectedEvent.severity].badge}`}>{SEVERITY_CONFIG[selectedEvent.severity].label}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-at-muted">{selectedEvent.eventType.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>
            </div>

            {/* Entity header */}
            <div className="at-card p-3">
              <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Source IP</div>
              <div className="text-lg font-mono text-at-accent">{selectedEvent.srcIp}</div>
            </div>

            {/* Details grid */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">Port</div>
                <div className="text-sm font-mono text-at-text">{selectedEvent.dstPort}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">Honeypot</div>
                <div className="text-sm text-at-text">{selectedEvent.honeypot}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">Country</div>
                <div className="text-sm text-at-text">{selectedEvent.country}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">ASN</div>
                <div className="text-sm font-mono text-at-text">{selectedEvent.asn || 'â€”'}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">Username</div>
                <div className="text-sm font-mono text-at-text">{selectedEvent.username || 'â€”'}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider">Timestamp</div>
                <div className="text-xs font-mono text-at-text">{new Date(selectedEvent.timestamp).toLocaleString()}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Command */}
            {selectedEvent.command && (
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Command Executed</div>
                <div className="at-card p-3 bg-at-subtle font-mono text-xs text-sev-high break-all leading-relaxed">
                  <Terminal size={12} className="inline mr-1 text-at-muted" />{selectedEvent.command}

            )}

            {/* Actions */}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Network, Search, Save, Download, Filter, LayoutDashboard, Maximize,
  ZoomIn, ZoomOut, RotateCcw, Crosshair, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft,
  Server, Globe, Hash, ShieldAlert, Users, Layers, AlertTriangle,
  Clock, Activity, FileText, ChevronDown, Lock, Shield, Eye
} from 'lucide-react';

type NodeType = 'ip' | 'domain' | 'hash' | 'actor' | 'host' | 'cve' | 'mitre';

interface GraphNode {
  id: string;
  type: NodeType;
  label: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  severity?: 'critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
}

interface GraphEdge {
  id: string;
  source: string;
  target: string;
  label: string;
  critical?: boolean;
}

const MOCK_NODES: GraphNode[] = [
  { id: 'n1', type: 'actor', label: 'APT28 (Fancy Bear)', x: 15, y: 50, severity: 'critical' },
  { id: 'n2', type: 'domain', label: 'c2-control-api.ru', x: 35, y: 30, severity: 'critical' },
  { id: 'n3', type: 'ip', label: '185.220.101.42', x: 35, y: 70, severity: 'high' },
  { id: 'n4', type: 'hash', label: 'payload_drop.exe', x: 55, y: 50, severity: 'critical' },
  { id: 'n5', type: 'host', label: 'SRV-DB-01', x: 75, y: 35 },
  { id: 'n6', type: 'host', label: 'SRV-WEB-04', x: 75, y: 65 },
  { id: 'n7', type: 'mitre', label: 'T1059.001 (PowerShell)', x: 90, y: 50 },
];

const MOCK_EDGES: GraphEdge[] = [
  { id: 'e1', source: 'n1', target: 'n2', label: 'operates' },
  { id: 'e2', source: 'n1', target: 'n3', label: 'owns' },
  { id: 'e3', source: 'n2', target: 'n4', label: 'hosts payload', critical: true },
  { id: 'e4', source: 'n3', target: 'n4', label: 'delivers', critical: true },
  { id: 'e5', source: 'n4', target: 'n5', label: 'executed on', critical: true },
  { id: 'e6', source: 'n4', target: 'n6', label: 'attempted on' },
  { id: 'e7', source: 'n5', target: 'n7', label: 'technique mapped' },
];

const ICONS: Record<NodeType, React.ElementType> = {
  ip: Server,
  domain: Globe,
  hash: Hash,
  actor: Users,
  host: Server,
  cve: ShieldAlert,
  mitre: Layers,
};

const TYPE_LABELS: Record<NodeType, string> = {
  ip: 'IP Address',
  domain: 'Domain Name',
  hash: 'File Hash',
  actor: 'Threat Actor',
  host: 'Target Host',
  cve: 'Vulnerability',
  mitre: 'ATT&CK Technique',
};

const TIMELINE_EVENTS = [
  { time: '14:22:11', entity: '185.220.101.42', event: 'Connection Established', type: 'network', sev: 'medium' },
  { time: '14:23:05', entity: 'c2-control-api.ru', event: 'DNS Resolution', type: 'network', sev: 'medium' },
  { time: '14:25:44', entity: 'payload_drop.exe', event: 'File Dropped', type: 'endpoint', sev: 'critical' },
  { time: '14:26:10', entity: 'SRV-DB-01', event: 'Process Execution', type: 'endpoint', sev: 'critical' },
  { time: '14:26:15', entity: 'T1059.001', event: 'PowerShell Invoked', type: 'detection', sev: 'high' },
];

export default function InvestigationPage() {
  const [selectedNodeId, setSelectedNodeId] = useState<string | null>('n4');
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState('Overview');

  const selectedNode = useMemo(() => MOCK_NODES.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId) || null, [selectedNodeId]);

  const connectedNodes = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedNodeId) return new Set<string>();
    const connected = new Set<string>([selectedNodeId]);
    MOCK_EDGES.forEach(e => {
      if (e.source === selectedNodeId) connected.add(e.target);
      if (e.target === selectedNodeId) connected.add(e.source);
    });
    return connected;
  }, [selectedNodeId]);

  const getNodeClass = (node: GraphNode) => {
    if (!selectedNodeId) return 'border-at-border bg-at-surface text-at-text';
    if (node.id === selectedNodeId) return 'border-at-accent bg-at-accent/10 text-white shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(214,40,40,0.3)] ring-1 ring-at-accent/50';
    if (connectedNodes.has(node.id)) return 'border-at-border bg-at-surface text-at-text';
    return 'border-at-border/30 bg-at-surface/50 text-at-muted opacity-40';
  };

  const getEdgeClass = (edge: GraphEdge) => {
    if (!selectedNodeId) return edge.critical ? 'stroke-at-accent/80' : 'stroke-at-muted';
    if (edge.source === selectedNodeId || edge.target === selectedNodeId) {
      return edge.critical ? 'stroke-at-accent' : 'stroke-at-text-secondary';
    }
    return 'stroke-at-disabled/30';
  };

  const getEdgeStroke = (edge: GraphEdge) => edge.critical ? 'rgba(214,40,40,0.8)' : 'rgba(130,130,130,0.5)';

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 bg-[#060606]">
      {/* TOP TOOLBAR */}
      <div className="h-[52px] border-b border-at-border bg-at-bg/90 flex items-center justify-between px-4 shrink-0 z-10 backdrop-blur-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-r border-at-border pr-4">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-at-accent/10 border border-at-accent/30 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Network size={16} className="text-at-accent" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-[13px] font-semibold text-at-text leading-tight">INV-2901: Cobalt Strike Beacon</h1>
              <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-muted flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-at-accent"></span> Active Investigation
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="relative">
            <Search size={14} className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-at-disabled" />
            <input placeholder="Search graph entities..." className="at-input w-64 pl-8 text-[12px] h-8 bg-at-surface" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center bg-at-surface border border-at-border rounded p-0.5">
            <button className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-at-subtle text-at-muted hover:text-at-text transition-colors" title="Zoom In"><ZoomIn size={14} /></button>
            <button className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-at-subtle text-at-muted hover:text-at-text transition-colors" title="Fit to Screen"><Maximize size={14} /></button>
            <button className="p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-at-subtle text-at-muted hover:text-at-text transition-colors" title="Zoom Out"><ZoomOut size={14} /></button>
          </div>
          <div className="w-px h-5 bg-at-border mx-1" />

          <button className="at-btn at-btn-ghost h-8 px-3 text-[12px]"><Download size={14} /> Export</button>
