        <div className="flex-1 relative bg-[#0a0a0a] overflow-hidden group/canvas" onClick={() => setSelectedNodeId(null)}>
          {/* Subtle Background Grid */}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none" style={{ backgroundImage: 'linear-gradient(rgba(255,255,255,0.03) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.03) 1px, transparent 1px)', backgroundSize: '40px 40px' }} />

          {/* SVG Edges */}
          <svg className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full pointer-events-none">
            <defs>
              <marker id="arrowhead" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" refX="28" refY="3" orient="auto">
                <polygon points="0 0, 6 3, 0 6" fill="rgba(150,150,150,0.5)" />
              </marker>
              <marker id="arrowhead-critical" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" refX="28" refY="3" orient="auto">
                <polygon points="0 0, 6 3, 0 6" fill="rgba(214,40,40,0.8)" />
              </marker>
            </defs>
            {MOCK_EDGES.map(edge => {
              const src = MOCK_NODES.find(n => n.id === edge.source);
              const tgt = MOCK_NODES.find(n => n.id === edge.target);
              if (!src || !tgt) return null;
              
              const isFaded = selectedNodeId && edge.source !== selectedNodeId && edge.target !== selectedNodeId;
              const strokeColor = getEdgeStroke(edge);
              const opacity = isFaded ? 0.15 : 1;
              const markerId = edge.critical ? 'url(#arrowhead-critical)' : 'url(#arrowhead)';

              return (
                <g key={edge.id} style={{ opacity, transition: 'opacity 0.3s' }}>
                  <line
                    x1={`${src.x}%`} y1={`${src.y}%`}
                    x2={`${tgt.x}%`} y2={`${tgt.y}%`}
                    stroke={strokeColor}
                    strokeWidth={edge.critical && !isFaded ? 2 : 1}
                    markerEnd={markerId}
                    strokeDasharray={edge.critical ? 'none' : '4,4'}
                  />
                  {/* Edge Label */}
                  <text
                    x={`${(src.x + tgt.x) / 2}%`} y={`${(src.y + tgt.y) / 2}%`}
                    fill={edge.critical ? '#d62828' : '#888'}
                    fontSize="10"
                    textAnchor="middle"
                    dy="-6"
                    className="font-mono bg-at-bg"
                  >
                    {edge.label}
                  </text>
                </g>
              );
            })}
          </svg>

          {/* Nodes */}
          {MOCK_NODES.map(node => {
            const Icon = ICONS[node.type];
            return (
              <div
                key={node.id}
                onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setSelectedNodeId(node.id); }}
                className={`absolute -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer group transition-all duration-300 z-10 hover:z-20`}
                style={{ left: `${node.x}%`, top: `${node.y}%` }}
              >
                <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${getNodeClass(node)}`}>
                  <Icon size={20} className={node.id === selectedNodeId ? 'text-white' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'} />
                </div>
                <div className={`text-[11px] font-mono whitespace-nowrap bg-at-bg/80 backdrop-blur px-2 py-0.5 rounded border ${node.id === selectedNodeId ? 'border-at-accent text-white' : 'border-at-border text-at-text-secondary'}`}>
                  {node.label}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* BOTTOM TIMELINE STRIP */}
          <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 right-4 bg-at-elevated/95 backdrop-blur-md border border-at-border rounded-lg shadow-2xl flex flex-col max-h-[140px]">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2 border-b border-at-border/50 shrink-0">
              <h3 className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-muted uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5"><Clock size={12} /> Investigation Timeline</h3>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button className="p-0.5 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text transition-colors"><Filter size={12} /></button>
                <button className="p-0.5 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text transition-colors"><ChevronDown size={12} /></button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden custom-scrollbar flex items-center p-3 gap-6 relative">
              <div className="absolute top-1/2 left-0 right-0 h-px bg-at-border -translate-y-1/2 z-0" />
              {TIMELINE_EVENTS.map((ev, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer w-32 shrink-0">
                  <div className="text-[9px] font-mono text-at-muted group-hover:text-at-text-secondary transition-colors">{ev.time}</div>
                  <div className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full border-2 border-[#060606] shadow-sm transition-transform group-hover:scale-125 ${
                    ev.sev === 'critical' ? 'bg-at-accent' : ev.sev === 'high' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-at-muted'
                  }`} />
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-medium text-center text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text line-clamp-1">{ev.event}</div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] font-mono text-at-disabled truncate w-full text-center">{ev.entity}</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RIGHT INSPECTOR */}
        {selectedNode ? (
          <div className="w-[340px] border-l border-at-border bg-at-bg/80 shrink-0 flex flex-col backdrop-blur-md z-10 animate-slide-left shadow-[-8px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)]">
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-at-border">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${selectedNode.severity === 'critical' ? 'bg-at-accent/10 border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'bg-at-surface border-at-border text-at-text'}`}>
                  {(() => { const Icon = ICONS[selectedNode.type]; return <Icon size={20} />; })()}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">{TYPE_LABELS[selectedNode.type]}</div>
                  <div className="text-[14px] font-mono font-medium text-at-text truncate" title={selectedNode.label}>{selectedNode.label}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {selectedNode.severity && (
                  <span className={`at-badge ${selectedNode.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : 'at-badge-high'}`}>
                    {selectedNode.severity.toUpperCase()} RISK
                  </span>
                )}
                <span className="at-badge bg-at-surface text-at-muted border-at-border">{connectedNodes.size - 1} Relations</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Tabs */}
            <div className="flex border-b border-at-border px-2">
              {['Overview', 'Intelligence', 'Evidence'].map(tab => (
                <button
                  key={tab}
                  onClick={() => setInspectorTab(tab)}
                  className={`px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${inspectorTab === tab ? 'border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'border-transparent text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}
                >
                  {tab}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Content */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5">
              {inspectorTab === 'Overview' && (
                <div className="space-y-5">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">First Seen</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">2026-09-21</div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">Last Seen</div>
                      <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">2 mins ago</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  {selectedNode.type === 'ip' && (
                    <>
                      <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">Location</div>
                        <div className="text-[12px] text-at-text flex items-center gap-2"><Globe size={12} className="text-at-muted" /> Moscow, Russia</div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">ASN</div>
                        <div className="text-[12px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">AS207656 (Hosting LLC)</div>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}

                  {selectedNode.type === 'hash' && (
                    <>
                      <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
                        <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">Detection Ratio</div>
                        <div className="text-[12px] font-bold text-at-accent flex items-center gap-2">45 / 72 <span className="text-[10px] font-normal text-at-muted">VirusTotal</span></div>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  )}

                  <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-2">Tags</div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {['malicious', 'c2', 'cobalt-strike'].map(t => (
                        <span key={t} className="at-chip text-[10px] bg-at-subtle text-at-text-secondary">{t}</span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Actions */}
            <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50">
              <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary flex-1 text-[11px] justify-center"><Search size={12} /> Pivot Search</button>
              <button className="at-btn at-btn-ghost w-8 p-0 flex items-center justify-center text-at-muted hover:text-at-accent"><Shield size={14} /></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="w-[340px] border-l border-at-border bg-at-bg/50 shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-8 text-center text-at-muted">
            <Crosshair size={32} className="mb-4 opacity-50" />
            <div className="text-[13px] font-medium text-at-text-secondary mb-1">No Entity Selected</div>
            <div className="text-[11px]">Select a node on the graph to inspect properties and context.</div>
          </div>
        )}
        
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Download, Plus, Globe, Hash, Server, Link2, ExternalLink, Activity, Network, Target } from 'lucide-react';
import { MOCK_IOCS, type MockIoc } from '../data/mockData';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

const TYPE_ICONS: Record<string, any> = { ip: Server, domain: Globe, hash: Hash, url: Link2 };

export default function IocPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [advancedMode, setAdvancedMode] = useState(false);
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>(['severity:critical']);
  const [selectedIocId, setSelectedIocId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState('Overview');

  const selectedIoc = useMemo(() => MOCK_IOCS.find(i => i.id === selectedIocId) || null, [selectedIocId]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_IOCS.filter(ioc => {
      const matchSearch = !search || ioc.value.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      const matchSev = activeFilters.includes('severity:critical') ? ioc.severity === 'critical' : true;
      return matchSearch && matchSev;
    });
  }, [search, activeFilters]);

  const removeFilter = (f: string) => setActiveFilters(prev => prev.filter(x => x !== f));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-12">Type</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Indicator</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Severity</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Source</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Last Seen</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Tags</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(ioc => {
          const Icon = TYPE_ICONS[ioc.type] || Server;
          const isSelected = selectedIocId === ioc.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={ioc.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIocId(ioc.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                <div className={`p-1.5 rounded inline-flex ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/20 text-at-accent' : 'bg-at-surface border border-at-border text-at-muted group-hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}>
                  <Icon size={12} />
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                <span className={`font-mono text-[12px] ${isSelected ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>{ioc.value}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                <span className={`at-badge ${ioc.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : ioc.severity === 'high' ? 'at-badge-high' : 'at-badge-medium'}`}>
                  {ioc.severity}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] text-at-muted">{ioc.source}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted">{ioc.lastSeen.split('T')[0]}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  {ioc.tags.slice(0, 2).map(t => <span key={t} className="at-chip text-[9px] bg-at-bg border-transparent">{t}</span>)}
                  {ioc.tags.length > 2 && <span className="at-chip text-[9px] bg-at-bg border-transparent">+{ioc.tags.length - 2}</span>}
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  const inspector = selectedIoc ? (
    <>
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-at-border relative">
        <button onClick={() => setSelectedIocId(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text"><X size={14} /></button>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded border border-at-accent bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
             {(() => { const Icon = TYPE_ICONS[selectedIoc.type] || Server; return <Icon size={20} />; })()}
          </div>
          <div className="pr-4 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-0.5">{selectedIoc.type}</div>
            <div className="text-[14px] font-mono font-medium text-at-text truncate">{selectedIoc.value}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`at-badge ${selectedIoc.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : 'at-badge-high'}`}>{selectedIoc.severity.toUpperCase()} RISK</span>
          <span className="at-badge bg-at-surface text-at-muted border-at-border">{selectedIoc.source}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex border-b border-at-border px-2">
        {['Overview', 'Timeline', 'Relationships'].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab}
            onClick={() => setInspectorTab(tab)}
            className={`px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${inspectorTab === tab ? 'border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'border-transparent text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}
          >
            {tab}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-5">
        {inspectorTab === 'Overview' && (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">First Seen</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{selectedIoc.firstSeen.split('T')[0]}</div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1">Last Seen</div>
                <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{selectedIoc.lastSeen.split('T')[0]}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-2">Intelligence Tags</div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {selectedIoc.tags.map(t => (
                  <span key={t} className="at-chip text-[10px] bg-at-subtle text-at-text-secondary">{t}</span>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-4">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Automated Actions</div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <button className="w-full text-left p-2 rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border hover:border-at-muted transition-colors flex items-center justify-between group">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Target size={12} className="text-at-muted" /><span className="text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text">Hunt in environment</span></div>
                  <ExternalLink size={12} className="text-at-disabled" />
                </button>
                <button className="w-full text-left p-2 rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border hover:border-at-muted transition-colors flex items-center justify-between group">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Network size={12} className="text-at-muted" /><span className="text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text">Pivot to Graph</span></div>
                  <ExternalLink size={12} className="text-at-disabled" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        
        {inspectorTab === 'Timeline' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-32 text-center text-at-muted">
            <Activity size={24} className="mb-2 opacity-30" />
            <div className="text-[11px]">No timeline events recorded yet.</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50">
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary w-full text-[11px] justify-center">View Full Report</button>
      </div>
    </>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <IntelLayout
      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Intelligence' }, { label: 'IOC' }]}
      title="Indicators of Compromise"
      description="Manage and analyze IPs, domains, hashes, and URLs."
      headerActions={
        <>
          <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> STIX</button>
          <button className="at-btn at-btn-primary at-btn-sm"><Plus size={13} /> Add IOC</button>
        </>
      }
      searchValue={search}
      onSearchChange={setSearch}
      searchPlaceholder="Search indicators, tags, sources..."
      activeFilters={activeFilters}
      onRemoveFilter={removeFilter}
      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedIocId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedIocId(null)}
      advancedMode={advancedMode}
      onToggleAdvanced={() => setAdvancedMode(!advancedMode)}
    />
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Download, Filter, Search, Layers, X, Target, Info, Shield } from 'lucide-react';
import PageHeader from '../components/layout/PageHeader';

const TACTICS = [
  'Initial Access', 'Execution', 'Persistence', 'Privilege Escalation', 'Defense Evasion',
  'Credential Access', 'Discovery', 'Lateral Movement', 'Collection', 'Command and Control',
  'Exfiltration', 'Impact'
];

const MOCK_TECHNIQUES = Array.from({ length: 60 }).map((_, i) => ({
  id: `T1${i.toString().padStart(3, '0')}`,
  name: `Technique ${i}`,
  tactic: TACTICS[i % TACTICS.length],
  coverage: Math.random() > 0.7 ? 'high' : Math.random() > 0.4 ? 'medium' : 'low'
}));

export default function MitreNavigatorPage() {
  const [selectedTech, setSelectedTech] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 bg-[#060606]">
      <PageHeader
        breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Detection & Response' }, { label: 'MITRE Navigator' }]}
        title="ATT&CK Navigator"
        description="Visualize threat actor techniques and detection coverage."
        actions={
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button className="at-btn at-btn-ghost at-btn-sm"><Filter size={13} /> Filter View</button>
            <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> Export Matrix</button>
          </div>
        }
      />
      
      {/* Workspace */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 min-h-0 relative">
        
        {/* Matrix Centerpiece */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto p-4 custom-scrollbar bg-at-bg">
          <div className="inline-flex gap-2 min-w-max">
            {TACTICS.map(tactic => {
              const tacticsTechs = MOCK_TECHNIQUES.filter(t => t.tactic === tactic);
              return (
                <div key={tactic} className="w-[180px] flex flex-col gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <div className="bg-at-surface border border-at-border p-2 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold text-at-text text-center shadow-sm sticky top-0 z-10 truncate" title={tactic}>
                    {tactic}
                    <div className="text-[9px] font-normal text-at-muted mt-0.5">{tacticsTechs.length} techniques</div>
                  </div>
                  
                  {tacticsTechs.map(tech => (
                    <div
                      key={tech.id}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedTech(tech.id)}
                      className={`p-2 rounded border cursor-pointer transition-colors text-[10px] flex flex-col gap-1
                        ${selectedTech === tech.id ? 'border-at-accent bg-at-accent/10 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(214,40,40,0.15)] ring-1 ring-at-accent' : 
                          'border-at-border bg-[#0a0a0a] hover:border-at-muted hover:bg-at-surface'
                        }
                      `}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className={`font-mono ${selectedTech === tech.id ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-text-secondary'}`}>{tech.id}</span>
                        <div className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${tech.coverage === 'high' ? 'bg-emerald-500' : tech.coverage === 'medium' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-at-bg border border-at-border'}`} title={`Coverage: ${tech.coverage}`} />
                      </div>
                      <div className={`truncate font-medium ${selectedTech === tech.id ? 'text-at-text' : 'text-at-muted'}`}>{tech.name}</div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {/* Detail Flyout */}
        {selectedTech && (
          <div className="w-[320px] shrink-0 border-l border-at-border bg-at-surface shadow-[-8px_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.5)] flex flex-col animate-slide-left z-20 h-full relative">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-at-border">

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { Download, Newspaper, ExternalLink, Hash, Clock, Tag, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { MOCK_NEWS } from '../data/mockData';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

export default function NewsPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [advancedMode, setAdvancedMode] = useState(false);
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedNewsId, setSelectedNewsId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [inspectorTab, setInspectorTab] = useState('Overview');

  const selectedNews = useMemo(() => MOCK_NEWS.find(n => n.id === selectedNewsId) || null, [selectedNewsId]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    return MOCK_NEWS.filter(news => {
      const matchSearch = !search || news.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());
      return matchSearch;
    });
  }, [search, activeFilters]);

  const removeFilter = (f: string) => setActiveFilters(prev => prev.filter(x => x !== f));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-16">Time</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-[40%]">Headline</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Source</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Category</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Relevance</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(news => {
          const isSelected = selectedNewsId === news.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={news.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedNewsId(news.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-4 text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted whitespace-nowrap">
                {new Date(news.published).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-4">
                <div className={`text-[13px] font-medium leading-snug line-clamp-2 ${isSelected ? 'text-at-text' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>
                  {news.title}
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-4">
                <span className="at-badge bg-at-surface text-at-muted border-at-border">{news.source}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-4">
                <span className="text-[11px] text-at-muted flex items-center gap-1.5"><Tag size={12} className="text-at-disabled"/> {news.category}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-4">
                <div className="w-16 h-1 bg-at-subtle rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="h-full bg-at-accent" style={{ width: `${Math.floor(Math.random() * 60 + 40)}%` }} />
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  const inspector = selectedNews ? (
    <>
      <div className="p-5 border-b border-at-border relative">
        <button onClick={() => setSelectedNewsId(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-at-disabled hover:text-at-text"><X size={14} /></button>
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4 mb-4">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded border border-at-border bg-at-surface text-at-text flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Newspaper size={20} />
          </div>
          <div className="pr-4 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">{selectedNews.source}</div>
            <div className="text-[14px] font-medium text-at-text leading-snug">{selectedNews.title}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="at-badge bg-at-surface text-at-text-secondary border-at-border">{selectedNews.category}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex border-b border-at-border px-2 shrink-0">
        {['Overview', 'Entities'].map(tab => (
          <button
            key={tab}
            onClick={() => setInspectorTab(tab)}
            className={`px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-medium border-b-2 transition-colors ${inspectorTab === tab ? 'border-at-accent text-at-accent' : 'border-transparent text-at-muted hover:text-at-text-secondary'}`}
          >
            {tab}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-6">
        {inspectorTab === 'Overview' && (
          <>
            <div>
              <div className="text-[10px] text-at-disabled mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Clock size={12}/> Published</div>
              <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">{new Date(selectedNews.published).toLocaleString()}</div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="border-t border-at-border/50 pt-5">
              <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-3">AI Summary</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-at-text-secondary leading-relaxed bg-[#060606] p-3 rounded border border-at-border/50">
                This article details a recent development involving {selectedNews.category.toLowerCase()} activities reported by {selectedNews.source}. The threat primarily affects enterprise networks and utilizes advanced techniques to bypass standard monitoring.
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        
        {inspectorTab === 'Entities' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted mb-2">Entities extracted from article:</div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">185.220.101.42</span>
                <span className="at-badge bg-at-bg text-at-muted">IP</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary">APT28</span>
                <span className="at-badge bg-at-bg text-at-muted">Actor</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50 space-y-2">
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary w-full text-[11px] justify-center flex items-center gap-2">Read Original <ExternalLink size={12} /></button>
      </div>
    </>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <IntelLayout
      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Intelligence' }, { label: 'News' }]}
      title="Threat Intelligence Feed"
      description="Aggregated and normalized security news, advisories, and research."
      headerActions={
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary at-btn-sm"><Download size={13} /> Export Report</button>
      }
      searchValue={search}
      onSearchChange={setSearch}
      searchPlaceholder="Search headlines, sources, or categories..."
      activeFilters={activeFilters}
      onRemoveFilter={removeFilter}
      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedNewsId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedNewsId(null)}
      advancedMode={advancedMode}
      onToggleAdvanced={() => setAdvancedMode(!advancedMode)}
    />
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Download, Plus, FileCode, CheckCircle, Shield, Code, Play } from 'lucide-react';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

interface MockRule {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  severity: 'critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
  status: 'active' | 'testing' | 'disabled';
  mitre: string[];
  format: 'sigma' | 'yara' | 'snort';
  lastModified: string;
  testStatus: 'passing' | 'failing' | 'untested';
}

const MOCK_RULES: MockRule[] = [
  { id: 'RUL-001', name: 'Suspicious PowerShell Download', severity: 'high', status: 'active', mitre: ['T1059.001'], format: 'sigma', lastModified: '2026-09-20', testStatus: 'passing' },
  { id: 'RUL-002', name: 'Cobalt Strike Beacon HTTP', severity: 'critical', status: 'active', mitre: ['T1071.001', 'T1573'], format: 'snort', lastModified: '2026-09-22', testStatus: 'passing' },
  { id: 'RUL-003', name: 'Mimikatz Execution Pattern', severity: 'critical', status: 'testing', mitre: ['T1003.001'], format: 'sigma', lastModified: '2026-09-24', testStatus: 'failing' },
  { id: 'RUL-004', name: 'Malicious Office Macro', severity: 'medium', status: 'active', mitre: ['T1059.005'], format: 'yara', lastModified: '2026-09-15', testStatus: 'passing' },
  { id: 'RUL-005', name: 'RDP Brute Force Attempt', severity: 'high', status: 'disabled', mitre: ['T1110.001'], format: 'sigma', lastModified: '2026-09-10', testStatus: 'untested' },
];

export default function RulesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedRuleId, setSelectedRuleId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selectedRule = MOCK_RULES.find(r => r.id === selectedRuleId) || null;
  const filtered = MOCK_RULES.filter(r => !search || r.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest w-12">Format</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Rule Name</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Severity</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Status</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">MITRE</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Test Status</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest text-right">Modified</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(rule => {
          const isSelected = selectedRuleId === rule.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={rule.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedRuleId(rule.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase text-at-muted bg-at-surface border border-at-border px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{rule.format}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className={`text-[12px] font-medium ${isSelected ? 'text-at-text' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>{rule.name}</div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`at-badge ${rule.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : rule.severity === 'high' ? 'at-badge-high' : 'at-badge-medium'}`}>{rule.severity}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-at-muted">
                  <div className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${rule.status === 'active' ? 'bg-at-accent' : rule.status === 'testing' ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-at-disabled'}`} />
                  <span className="capitalize">{rule.status}</span>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex gap-1 flex-wrap">
                  {rule.mitre.map(t => <span key={t} className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-text-secondary bg-[#060606] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{t}</span>)}
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 ${rule.testStatus === 'passing' ? 'text-emerald-500' : rule.testStatus === 'failing' ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-muted'}`}>
                  {rule.testStatus === 'passing' ? <CheckCircle size={10} /> : rule.testStatus === 'failing' ? <Shield size={10} /> : null}
                  {rule.testStatus}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-mono text-at-muted text-right">{rule.lastModified}</td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );

  const inspector = selectedRule ? (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-at-bg">
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-at-border flex items-center justify-between bg-at-surface/30">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-at-surface border border-at-border flex items-center justify-center text-at-text"><FileCode size={14} /></div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-[14px] font-medium text-at-text leading-tight">{selectedRule.name}</div>
            <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-muted">{selectedRule.id}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* Editor Surface */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col p-4 bg-[#0a0a0a]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest flex items-center gap-1.5"><Code size={12}/> Rule Definition</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-at-muted bg-at-surface px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">{selectedRule.format.toUpperCase()}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 border border-at-border rounded bg-[#060606] p-4 overflow-y-auto font-mono text-[12px] text-at-text-secondary leading-relaxed custom-scrollbar">
          <span className="text-at-muted">title:</span> Suspicious PowerShell Download<br/>
          <span className="text-at-muted">id:</span> 34a2e5d5-24b5-412f-9815-f864459f2a24<br/>
          <span className="text-at-muted">status:</span> experimental<br/>
          <span className="text-at-muted">description:</span> Detects suspicious PowerShell download cradle<br/>
          <span className="text-at-muted">logsource:</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">product:</span> windows<br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">service:</span> powershell<br/>
          <span className="text-at-muted">detection:</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">selection:</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">EventID:</span> 4104<br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">ScriptBlockText|contains:</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;- <span className="text-amber-500">'Net.WebClient'</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;- <span className="text-amber-500">'DownloadString'</span><br/>
          &nbsp;&nbsp;<span className="text-at-muted">condition:</span> selection<br/>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Editor Actions */}
      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border bg-at-surface/50 flex gap-2 shrink-0">
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-secondary flex-1 text-[11px] justify-center"><Play size={12}/> Run Tests</button>
        <button className="at-btn at-btn-primary flex-1 text-[11px] justify-center">Save Rule</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <IntelLayout
      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Detection & Response' }, { label: 'Rules' }]}
      title="Detection Engineering"
      description="Manage, author, and test detection rules across the environment."
      headerActions={<button className="at-btn at-btn-primary at-btn-sm"><Plus size={13} /> New Rule</button>}
      searchValue={search}
      onSearchChange={setSearch}
      searchPlaceholder="Search rules, tags, or mitre tactics..."
      activeFilters={activeFilters}
      onRemoveFilter={f => setActiveFilters(prev => prev.filter(x => x !== f))}
      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedRuleId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedRuleId(null)}
    />
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Download, Plus, ShieldAlert, CheckCircle, Clock, Server, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import IntelLayout from '../components/layout/IntelLayout';

interface MockVuln {
  id: string;
  cve: string;
  asset: string;
  severity: 'critical' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'low';
  cvss: number;
  vendor: string;
  status: 'open' | 'patching' | 'mitigated' | 'resolved';
  remediation: string;
  assignee: string;
  lastSeen: string;
}

const MOCK_VULNS: MockVuln[] = [
  { id: 'V-991', cve: 'CVE-2024-21762', asset: 'FGT-GW-01', severity: 'critical', cvss: 9.6, vendor: 'Fortinet', status: 'patching', remediation: 'Upgrade to 7.4.3', assignee: 'SysAdmin', lastSeen: '1 hr ago' },
  { id: 'V-992', cve: 'CVE-2023-46805', asset: 'IVANTI-VPN', severity: 'critical', cvss: 9.1, vendor: 'Ivanti', status: 'open', remediation: 'Apply XML mitigation', assignee: 'SecOps', lastSeen: '4 hrs ago' },
  { id: 'V-993', cve: 'CVE-2024-3094', asset: 'UBUNTU-WEB-02', severity: 'high', cvss: 8.8, vendor: 'XZ Utils', status: 'mitigated', remediation: 'Downgrade to 5.6.0', assignee: 'DevOps', lastSeen: '1 day ago' },
  { id: 'V-994', cve: 'CVE-2021-44228', asset: 'SRV-JIRA-01', severity: 'medium', cvss: 6.5, vendor: 'Apache', status: 'resolved', remediation: 'Updated Log4j', assignee: 'SysAdmin', lastSeen: '3 days ago' },
];

export default function VulnerabilityManagerPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<string[]>(['status:open OR patching']);
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selected = MOCK_VULNS.find(v => v.id === selectedId) || null;
  const filtered = MOCK_VULNS.filter(v => !search || v.cve.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || v.asset.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

  const table = (
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
      <thead>
        <tr className="border-b border-at-border bg-at-surface/30 sticky top-0 z-10">
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">CVE</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Asset</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Severity (CVSS)</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Status</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Remediation</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest">Assigned</th>
          <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest text-right">Last Seen</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {filtered.map(vuln => {
          const isSelected = selectedId === vuln.id;
          return (
            <tr
              key={vuln.id}
              onClick={() => setSelectedId(vuln.id)}
              className={`border-b border-at-border/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${isSelected ? 'bg-at-accent/5' : 'hover:bg-white/[0.02]'}`}
            >
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`font-mono text-[12px] font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-at-text-secondary group-hover:text-at-text'}`}>{vuln.cve}</span>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary"><Server size={12} className="text-at-disabled"/> {vuln.asset}</div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className={`at-badge ${vuln.severity === 'critical' ? 'at-badge-critical' : vuln.severity === 'high' ? 'at-badge-high' : 'at-badge-medium'}`}>{vuln.severity}</span>
                  <span className={`font-mono text-[11px] ${vuln.cvss >= 9 ? 'text-at-accent' : 'text-amber-500'}`}>{vuln.cvss}</span>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <span className={`at-badge ${vuln.status === 'open' ? 'bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent border-at-accent/30' : vuln.status === 'patching' ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500 border-amber-500/30' : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-500 border-emerald-500/30'}`}>
                  {vuln.status.toUpperCase()}

              <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary">{vuln.assignee}</td>

  );

            <AlertTriangle size={20} />
          </div>
          <div className="pr-4 min-w-0">

            <div className="text-[14px] font-mono font-bold text-at-text truncate">{selected.cve}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className={`at-badge ${selected.status === 'open' ? 'bg-at-accent/10 text-at-accent' : selected.status === 'patching' ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-500'}`}>{selected.status.toUpperCase()}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex-1 p-5 overflow-y-auto space-y-5">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Remediation Action</div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-at-surface border border-at-border rounded text-[11px] text-at-text-secondary">
            {selected.remediation}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-at-disabled uppercase tracking-widest mb-2">Vendor Details</div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-at-muted flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#060606] p-2 rounded border border-at-border/50">
            {selected.vendor} - See advisory link
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="p-4 border-t border-at-border shrink-0 bg-at-surface/50 space-y-2">

  ) : null;

  return (

      breadcrumbs={[{ label: 'Detection & Response' }, { label: 'Vulnerability Manager' }]}

      resultsCount={filtered.length}
      tableContent={table}
      inspectorContent={inspector}
      isInspectorOpen={!!selectedId}
      onCloseInspector={() => setSelectedId(null)}
    />
  );
}
